# Agents · D · part 19

1,006 entries, doc-reader to dockerfile-generator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **doc-reader**: Read-only reader for the testkit skills. Reads requirements, specs, test files and code, and returns its findings as text. It has no shell, can't write files and can't use the web. ([ejlomuntad/qa-spectre](https://github.com/ejlomuntad/qa-spectre/blob/HEAD/plugins/testkit/agents/doc-reader.md))
- **doc-reader**: Read architecture documentation under docs/ for statements relevant to a question and return section-level locators with the documented intent. Use to compare documented architecture with implementation. ([GROOVIZ/code-atlas-plugin](https://github.com/GROOVIZ/code-atlas-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-reader.md))
- **doc-reader**: Reads long documents (PDF CV, LinkedIn export, job posting, annual report, member directory, large files in this repo) and extracts exactly what the brief asks for. Use to keep long reads out of the main context. Never writes files. ([samsam007b/carriere-kit](https://github.com/samsam007b/carriere-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-reader.md))
- **doc-readme**: Escreve ou reescreve o README.md do projeto (o que é, como rodar, configurar, testar, estrutura e links para o resto da documentação), com comandos verificados no repositório. Use quando o doc-seletor recomendar, quando não houver README ou quando ele estiver desatualizado. ([PedroWC/engenheiro-software](https://github.com/PedroWC/engenheiro-software/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-readme.md))
- **doc-recon**: Reads a codebase in isolation and returns a compact project map: what the project does, its main features, entry points, likely audiences, and candidate docs. Use to orient a from-scratch documentation effort without reading the whole repo in the main conversation. ([EdwardAngert/docs-agent-plugin](https://github.com/EdwardAngert/docs-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-recon.md) · ★5)
- **doc-reconcile**: Promotes a plan's staged doc claims into the feature specs and the protocol document, verifying each against the code before it lands. Use when the orchestrator invokes reconciliation after an approved review, or for ad-hoc doc upkeep outside the pipeline. Takes a plan name, or a changed-file list. ([Zalaras/muster](https://github.com/Zalaras/muster/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-reconcile.md) · ★3)
- **doc-reconciler**: Documentation truth reconciler. Detects drift between CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, README, nested CLAUDE.md files, and the active codebase. Maintains the surfaces index. Invoke after any interface boundary or module-map change. ([ianshank/Mouse-Droid-AGI](https://github.com/ianshank/Mouse-Droid-AGI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-reconciler.md) · ★1)
- **doc-reconciler**: Use PROACTIVELY at the end of any session that changed code, docs, scope or a decision — a session that ends without this pass has left the docs stale, the same defect as a missing changelog entry. Do not wait to be asked. Appends the WORKLOG, IMPLEMENTATION and DOC-REGISTRY entries for the change just made, and drafts the OPEN-WORK and INTERVIEW deltas for review. Never touches CLAUDE.md or docs/adr/. ([arpitarya/cage](https://github.com/arpitarya/cage/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/doc-reconciler.md))
- **doc-refactor**: Document refactoring expert. Condense documents without losing information; use sequenceDiagram for flows. ([sd0xdev/sd0x-harness](https://github.com/sd0xdev/sd0x-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-refactor.md) · ★196)
- **doc-refactorer**: Reestrutura e simplifica documentação quando o usuário pediu edição explícita. Use para transformar documentação confusa em versão clara, curta e executável, preservando riscos, comandos, nomes oficiais e informação crítica. ([tiagoyoko/doctail](https://github.com/tiagoyoko/doctail/blob/HEAD/agents/doc-refactorer.md))
- **doc-reference**: Subagent for generating code reference documentation from codeprobe index data and GitNexus code intelligence. ([laithalsaadoon/codeprobe](https://github.com/laithalsaadoon/codeprobe/blob/HEAD/src/codeprobe/bootstrap/templates/claude-plugin/agents/doc-reference.md))

- **dockerfile-ci-expert**: Use this agent when you need to create, modify, or optimize Dockerfiles, configure CI/CD pipelines (GitHub Actions, GitLab CI, Jenkins, etc.), set up container orchestration, troubleshoot build issues, or implement DevOps best practices. - **dockerfile-editor**: Makes a single targeted edit to a Dockerfile or docker-compose.yml. Adds a layer, updates a base image, adds a service, or edits environment configuration. - **dockerfile-fixer**: Applies exactly the fixes listed in the fix plan (and any fix-reviewer REDO instructions) to the Dockerfile or dependency files.
